### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「届いたことを確認し、順番どおり確実に届ける」通信、UDPは「確認や順番の管理をせず、速さを優先して送る」通信です。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para limpiar tu rama local antes de compartirla: ordenar, unir o reescribir tus commits que aún no están en remoto. Usa merge para integrar una rama que ya es pública o compartida, porque conserva su historia tal cual y no obliga a nadie a reescribir nada.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是面向连接、可靠的传输：保证数据有序、不丢不重地送到；UDP 是无连接、不保证可靠的传输：只管尽快发出去，丢了不补。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when the branch is still yours alone and you want a clean, linear history before sharing it. Merge when others already have the branch or you want the real history of parallel work kept as it happened.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用 `bun test` 后面直接跟文件路径就行：

```bash
bun test tests/foo.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配名字里含有这段文字的测试文件：

```bash
bun test foo
```

如果想只跑某个 `test(...)` 用例，用 `-t` 按用例名过滤：

```bash
bun test -t "用例名的一部分"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where every change to the database gets written down before it is applied to the actual data. If the system crashes midway, the log can be replayed to restore the changes that were committed but not yet saved to the main storage.
```

      if (t.price >= 600) val.style.color = 'var(--d-muted)';
      li.appendChild(main);
      li.appendChild(subEl);
      li.appendChild(val);
      list.appendChild(li);
    });

  var init = { when: '第 3 天', trains: [

    { no: 'G7', price: 626, left: '' }

  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('latest')).then(function (d) {
    render(d || init);
  });
  ducter.onChange(function () {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('latest')).then(render);
  });
})();

<script>
function fmt(n){return "¥"+Math.round(n).toLocaleString("zh-CN");}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+(+x.yuan||0),0);
  const t=document.getElementById("total");
  const c=document.getElementById("count");
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  if(xs.length){
    t.textContent=fmt(total);
    c.textContent=xs.length+" 笔";
  }else{
    t.innerHTML='<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';
    c.textContent="还没有花销";
  }
  list.innerHTML="";
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");main.className="k-row-main";main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");sub.className="k-row-sub";sub.textContent=x.day;
    main.appendChild(sub);
    const val=document.createElement("div");val.className="k-row-value";val.textContent=fmt(x.yuan);
    row.appendChild(main);row.appendChild(val);list.appendChild(row);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total):"还没记");
}
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();

(function () {
  var items = [];

    if (it) $('sel-name').textContent = '选中:' + it.what + ' ' + money(it.amount);

  $('save').addEventListener('click', function () {

      what: what,
      amount: amount,
      date: $('date').value.trim(),
      from: ''
    });
    $('what').value = '';
    $('amt').value = '';
    $('date').value = '';
    save();
    render();
  });

  $('del').addEventListener('click', function () {
    items = items.filter(function (x) { return x.id !== selected; });
    selected = null;
    save();
    render();
  });

  function load(v) {
    items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    render();
  }

  ducter.onChange(function () { ducter.get('items').then(load); });
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('items')).then(load);
})();

  function load() {

      let parsed = v;
      if (typeof v === 'string') { try { parsed = JSON.parse(v); } catch (e) { parsed = []; } }
      items = Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [];

  const t = document.getElementById('time').value.trim();

  if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(t) || !(ml > 0)) return;
  feeds.push({ d: today(), t: t.padStart(5, '0'), ml });
  document.getElementById('ml').value = '';
  document.getElementById('time').value = nowHM();
  document.querySelectorAll('.k-chip').forEach(c => c.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false'));
  save();
});

document.getElementById('list').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
  if (!row) return;
  const i = Number(row.dataset.i);
  if (armed === i) {
    feeds.splice(i, 1);
    armed = null;
    save();
  } else {
    armed = i;
    render();
  }
});

ducter.onChange(load);
load();
